# David Webb Secondary Market — 2026-08-12

## Executive Summary

This is an initial baseline run of the David Webb secondary-market pipeline as of August 12, 2026; the 1,455 dealer listings flagged as "new" in the four-week window are overwhelmingly the product of a recent inventory backfill rather than any genuine acceleration in market activity, and should be read accordingly. With that context, the tracked universe stands at 1,447 active dealer listings at a median ask of $25,000, alongside a database of 3,884 auction records providing the pricing bedrock for valuation analysis. The most recent auction window — 19 confirmed sales over the past 60 days, median hammer of $32,000, led largely by Sotheby's June sessions — suggests steady but measured collector demand at the block. On the dealer side, the standout ask is a cabochon emerald and diamond bracelet offered by Eric Originals & Antiques at $410,000, closely followed by a Kentshire mid-century diamond cocktail ring at $375,000, both indicative of the premium commanded by signature Webb sculptural jewelry at the top of the market. Detailed breakdowns of current inventory, new listings, and closed transactions follow in the report sections below.

## Currently Available

_1,447 active listings across all tracked dealer marketplaces (estate jewelers, 1stDibs, Sotheby's Buy Now, The RealReal). This section is dealer inventory only — there is currently no live, biddable David Webb auction inventory to track; every auction-history record on file is a completed, historical sale (see "Recent Auction Sales" below)._

## Data-Quality Caveats

- **Auction hammer prices exclude buyer's premium** — typically +15–26% depending on the house.
- **Dealer asking prices are not confirmed sale prices** — they're the dealer's current ask, which may be negotiated down or never sell.
- **"Closed" (dealer status: inactive) usually but not always means sold** — could also be a price relist under a new SKU or a data-feed gap.
- **"Currently available" is dealer inventory only.** There is no live, biddable David Webb auction inventory being tracked right now — genuine upcoming auction lots were confirmed absent at the major houses as of the last investigation.
- **A piece can be listed on more than one platform** (e.g. a dealer's own site and 1stDibs) — "total listings" counts platform presence, not unique physical pieces.
- **Foreign-currency prices are converted to USD** using approximate historical annual-average exchange rates, not settlement-date-precise figures — the original native-currency amount is footnoted wherever it differs from the displayed USD figure.
